1. WinGo analysis mein use hone wale mathematical methods
1. Frequency Analysis
Pichhle 50, 100, 500 ya 1,000 results mein har number kitni baar aaya, ye count karna.
Example: 0 = 8 baar, 1 = 12 baar, 2 = 9 baar.
Kaam: Number distribution aur unusual frequencies dekhna.
2. Probability & Percentage
Kisi category ki historical frequency ko percentage mein badalna.
Formula: 
Kaam: Big/Small, colour ya number ki historical frequency compare karna.
3. Moving Window / Recent Trend
Sirf latest 10, 20 ya 50 rounds ka analysis karke usse longer history se compare karna.
Kaam: Recent distribution mein badlav identify karna; agla result guarantee nahi karna.
Streak & Transition Analysis
Jaise BIG → BIG → SMALL, ya kitni baar BIG ke baad SMALL aaya.
Kaam: Result sequences ka pattern measure karna. Independent draws mein streak khud se reversal ki guarantee nahi hoti.
5. Conditional Probability / Markov Analysis
Historical data mein check karna ki pichhle result ya pichhle do results ke baad kaunsa result kitni baar aaya.
Kaam: Sequence dependence test karna; sirf correlation milna kaafi nahi, out-of-sample validation bhi chahiye.
6. Statistical Significance
Chi-square test, confidence intervals aur expected frequency se check karna ki observed pattern random variation se alag hai ya nahi.
Kaam: False patterns ko genuine evidence samajhne ki galti kam karna.
7. Backtesting & Accuracy Validation
Purane data par model test karna, lekin har prediction ke waqt sirf us samay available data use karna. Phir unseen results par test repeat karna.
Kaam: Pata karna ki method simple baseline se behtar hai bhi ya nahi.
Step 2 — Data Validation
Duplicate periods, missing results aur incorrect records check karna
Step 3 — Statistical Features
Frequency, recent trend, streaks, transitions aur number distribution
Step 4 — Model & Probability
Baseline probability ke saath candidate predictions compare karna
Step 5 — Validation & Output
Backtest, measured accuracy, probability estimate aur prediction status

    'BBBSBS_S': ['SSSBSB Trend Injection 🎯', '🎯 SMALL×3 then BSB — BIG next!'],

    'ADAPTIVE': ['🧮 Adaptive Math', '🧮 Pure math prediction!'],
    'ACCELERATION_B': ['BIG Acceleration 📈', '📈 BIG streaks getting longer!'],

    'PRESSURE_S': ['SMALL Pressure ⬇️', '⬇️ SMALL will break through!'],
    'REBOUND_B': ['BIG Rebound ↩️', '↩️ BIG rebounded — will hold!'],

// ── Current prediction state ──
let currentPrediction = null;
let bunnyUpdateCount = 0;

// ── Helper: Convert number to B/S ──
function numToType(n) {
    return n >= 5 ? 'B' : 'S';
}

// ── Helper: Look up pattern in DB ──
function lookupPattern(pattern) {
    return CPU_PATTERN_DB[pattern] || null;
}

// ── Helper: Live adaptive detection (fallback) ──
function liveAdaptiveDetect(types) {
    if (types.length < 4) return null;
    const len = Math.min(types.length, 10);
    const slice = types.slice(0, len);
    const seq = slice.join('');

    // Try to find longest matching pattern in DB
    for (let l = Math.min(seq.length, 9); l >= 3; l--) {
        const sub = seq.substring(0, l);
        const hit = lookupPattern(sub);
        if (hit) {
            return {
                method: 'AI_LIVE',
                size: hit.next,
                conf: hit.conf
            };
        }
    }

    // Fallback: statistical
    const bCount = slice.filter(t => t === 'B').length;
    const total = slice.length;
    const bRatio = bCount / total;
    if (bRatio >= 0.7) {
        return { method: 'MATH', size: 'SMALL', conf: 65 + Math.floor(bRatio * 20) };
    } else if (bRatio <= 0.3) {
        return { method: 'MATH', size: 'BIG', conf: 65 + Math.floor((1 - bRatio) * 20) };
    }
    return null;
}

// ── Main Bunny AI Engine ──
function runBunnyAI(historyNumbers) {
    if (!historyNumbers || historyNumbers.length < 3) return;
    bunnyUpdateCount++;

    const hist10 = historyNumbers.slice(0, 10);
    const deepHist = historyNumbers.slice(0, 500);
    const types = deepHist.map(n => numToType(n));

    // ── Update dots (safe DOM) ──
    const dotsEl = document.getElementById('bunnyDots');
    if (dotsEl) {
        dotsEl.innerHTML = hist10.map((n, i) => {
            const isBig = n >= 5;
            const col = isBig ? '#ef4444' : '#00d4ff';
            const bg = isBig ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.15)' : 'rgba(0,212,255,0.15)';
            const label = isBig ? 'B' : 'S';
            const opacity = 1 - (i * 0.07);
            return `<div style="width:20px;height:20px;border-radius:50%;background:${bg};border:1.5px solid ${col};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:8px;font-weight:900;color:${col};opacity:${opacity};flex-shrink:0;">${label}</div>`;
        }).join('');
    }

    // ── Detect pattern ──
    let patKey = 'MULTI';
    let pred = currentPrediction ? currentPrediction.size : '--';
    let conf = currentPrediction ? currentPrediction.confidence : 70;

    // ── Streak detection ──
    let sk = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < types.length - 1 && types[i] === types[i + 1]; i++) sk++;
    const cur = types[0];
    const opp = cur === 'B' ? 'S' : 'B';

    // ── Cycle detection ──
    let cycleFound = false;
    for (let cl = 2; cl <= 8; cl++) {
        if (types.length < cl * 2) continue;
        let ok = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < cl; j++) {
            if (types[j] !== types[j + cl]) { ok = false; break; }
        }
        if (ok) {
            patKey = 'CYCLE_' + cl;
            cycleFound = true;
            conf = cl >= 5 ? 96 : cl >= 3 ? 91 : 82;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!cycleFound) {
        const b10 = types.slice(0, 10).filter(t => t === 'B').length;

        if (sk >= 6) {
            patKey = cur === 'B' ? 'DRAGON_B' : 'DRAGON_S';
            conf = Math.min(95, conf + 3);
            pred = cur === 'B' ? 'SMALL' : 'BIG';
        } else if (sk === 5) {
            patKey = cur === 'B' ? '5x_STREAK_B' : '5x_STREAK_S';
            conf = Math.min(93, conf + 2);
            pred = cur === 'B' ? 'SMALL' : 'BIG';
        } else if (sk >= 4) {
            patKey = '4x_STREAK_' + cur;
            conf = Math.min(92, conf + 1);
            pred = cur === 'B' ? 'SMALL' : 'BIG';
        } else if (sk === 3) {
            const p6 = types.length >= 6 ? types.slice(0, 6).join('') : '';
            const p7 = types.length >= 7 ? types.slice(0, 7).join('') : '';
            const p8 = types.length >= 8 ? types.slice(0, 8).join('') : '';

            if (p8 === 'BBBSBSBS' || p7 === 'BBBSBSB' || p6 === 'BBBSBS') {
                patKey = 'BBBSBS_B';
                conf = 80;
                pred = 'SMALL';
            } else if (p8 === 'SSSBSBSB' || p7 === 'SSSBSBS' || p6 === 'SSSBSB') {
                patKey = 'BBBSBS_S';
                conf = 80;
                pred = 'BIG';
            } else {
                patKey = '3x_STREAK_' + cur;
                conf = 78;
                pred = cur === 'B' ? 'SMALL' : 'BIG';
            }
        } else if (sk === 2) {
            patKey = '2x_STREAK_' + cur;
            conf = 65;
            pred = cur === 'B' ? 'SMALL' : 'BIG';
        } else {
            // ── Advanced pattern detection ──
            const seq6 = types.length >= 6 ? types.slice(0, 6).join('') : '';
            const seq7 = types.length >= 7 ? types.slice(0, 7).join('') : '';
            const seq8 = types.length >= 8 ? types.slice(0, 8).join('') : '';
            const seq9 = types.length >= 9 ? types.slice(0, 9).join('') : '';
            const seq10 = types.length >= 10 ? types.slice(0, 10).join('') : '';

            let found = false;

            // ── STUTTER / SINGLE BREAK ──
            if (seq8 === 'BBBBBBBS' || seq7 === 'BBBBBBS' || seq6 === 'BBBBBS') {
                patKey = 'STUTTER_B';
                conf = 86;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (seq8 === 'SSSSSSSB' || seq7 === 'SSSSSSB' || seq6 === 'SSSSSB') {
                patKey = 'STUTTER_S';
                conf = 86;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            } else if (seq6 === 'BBBBSB' || seq5 === 'BBBSB') {
                patKey = 'SINGLE_BREAK_B';
                conf = 79;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (seq6 === 'SSSSBS' || seq5 === 'SSSBS') {
                patKey = 'SINGLE_BREAK_S';
                conf = 79;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── TRAP ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BBSSBB' || seq7 === 'BBSSBB' || seq8 === 'BBSSBB')) {
                patKey = 'TRAP_B';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SSBBSS' || seq7 === 'SSBBSS' || seq8 === 'SSBBSS')) {
                patKey = 'TRAP_S';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── SANDWICH ──
            if (!found && seq5 === 'BBSBB') {
                patKey = 'SANDWICH_B';
                conf = 71;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && seq5 === 'SSBSS') {
                patKey = 'SANDWICH_S';
                conf = 71;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── PENDULUM ──
            if (!found && (seq7 === 'BBSSSBB' || seq8 === 'BBSSSBB')) {
                patKey = 'PENDULUM';
                conf = 78;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq7 === 'SSBBBSS' || seq8 === 'SSBBBSS')) {
                patKey = 'PENDULUM';
                conf = 78;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── TWIN PULSE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSBBSB' || seq5 === 'BSBBS')) {
                patKey = 'TWIN_PULSE';
                conf = 68;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBSSBS' || seq5 === 'SBSSB')) {
                patKey = 'TWIN_PULSE';
                conf = 68;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── WAVE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBBBSS' || seq7 === 'SBBBSS')) {
                patKey = 'WAVE_UP';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSSBB' || seq7 === 'BSSBB')) {
                patKey = 'WAVE_DOWN';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── MIRROR ──
            if (!found && seq6 === 'BBSSBB') {
                patKey = 'MIRROR';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && seq6 === 'SSBBSS') {
                patKey = 'MIRROR';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── FADE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBBBSS' || seq7 === 'SBBBSS')) {
                patKey = 'FADE_B';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSSBB' || seq7 === 'BSSBB')) {
                patKey = 'FADE_S';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── CLUSTER JUMP ──
            if (!found && (seq7 === 'BBBBSSS' || seq8 === 'BBBBSSS')) {
                patKey = 'CLUSTER_JUMP';
                conf = 88;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq7 === 'SSSSBBB' || seq8 === 'SSSSBBB')) {
                patKey = 'CLUSTER_JUMP';
                conf = 88;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ECHO ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSBSB' || seq7 === 'BSBSB')) {
                patKey = 'ECHO';
                conf = 65;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBSBS' || seq7 === 'SBSBS')) {
                patKey = 'ECHO';
                conf = 65;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ACCELERATION ──
            if (!found && (seq7 === 'BBBBBBB' || seq8 === 'BBBBBBB')) {
                patKey = 'ACCELERATION_B';
                conf = 92;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq7 === 'SSSSSSS' || seq8 === 'SSSSSSS')) {
                patKey = 'ACCELERATION_S';
                conf = 92;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── MOMENTUM ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BBBBBB' || seq5 === 'BBBBB')) {
                patKey = 'MOMENTUM_B';
                conf = 90;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SSSSSS' || seq5 === 'SSSSS')) {
                patKey = 'MOMENTUM_S';
                conf = 90;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── NOVA ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBBBBB' || seq5 === 'SBBBB')) {
                patKey = 'NOVA_B';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSSSSS' || seq5 === 'BSSSS')) {
                patKey = 'NOVA_S';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── REBOUND ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBBBBB' || seq5 === 'SBBBB')) {
                patKey = 'REBOUND_B';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSSSSS' || seq5 === 'BSSSS')) {
                patKey = 'REBOUND_S';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── PRESSURE ──
            if (!found && (seq8 === 'BBBBBBBB' || seq7 === 'BBBBBBB')) {
                patKey = 'PRESSURE_B';
                conf = 94;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq8 === 'SSSSSSSS' || seq7 === 'SSSSSSS')) {
                patKey = 'PRESSURE_S';
                conf = 94;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── TRIPLE BREAK ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSBSBS' || seq5 === 'BSBSB')) {
                patKey = 'TRIPLE_BREAK_B';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBSBSB' || seq5 === 'SBSBS')) {
                patKey = 'TRIPLE_BREAK_S';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ZIGZAG BLOCK ──
            if (!found && (seq8 === 'BBBSBSBS' || seq7 === 'BBBSBSB')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG_BLOCK_B';
                conf = 81;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq8 === 'SSSBSBSB' || seq7 === 'SSSBSBS')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG_BLOCK_S';
                conf = 81;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── HEAVY BIAS ──
            if (!found && b10 >= 7) {
                patKey = 'HEAVY_BIG';
                conf = 82;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && b10 <= 3) {
                patKey = 'HEAVY_SMALL';
                conf = 82;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ZIGZAG ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BSBSBS' || seq5 === 'BSBSB' || seq4 === 'BSBS')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SBSBSB' || seq5 === 'SBSBS' || seq4 === 'SBSB')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── DOUBLE DOUBLE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'BBSSBB' || seq5 === 'BBSSB')) {
                patKey = 'DOUBLE_DOUBLE';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'SMALL';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'SSBBSS' || seq5 === 'SSBBS')) {
                patKey = 'DOUBLE_DOUBLE';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'BIG';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── Fallback: adaptive ──
            if (!found) {
                const adapt = liveAdaptiveDetect(types);
                if (adapt) {
                    patKey = adapt.method === 'AI_LIVE' ? 'LIVE_LEARN' : 'ADAPTIVE';
                    conf = adapt.conf;
                    pred = adapt.size;
                    found = true;
                }
            }

            // ── Ultimate fallback ──
            if (!found) {
                patKey = 'CHAOS';
                conf = 55;
                pred = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'BIG' : 'SMALL';
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Look up pattern name ──
    const info = BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES[patKey] || ['Unknown', '🔍 Analyzing...'];
    const patName = info[0];
    let msg = info[1];

    const callCol = pred === 'BIG' ? '#ef4444' : pred === 'SMALL' ? '#00d4ff' : '#a855f7';
    const confCol = conf >= 85 ? '#22c55e' : conf >= 70 ? '#facc15' : '#f97316';

    // ── Update DOM ──
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('bunnyMsg');
    if (msgEl) msgEl.innerHTML = msg;

    const patEl = document.getElementById('bunnyPatName');
    if (patEl) { patEl.innerText = patName; }

    const confEl = document.getElementById('bunnyConf');
    if (confEl) { confEl.innerText = conf + '%';
        confEl.style.color = confCol; }

    const callEl = document.getElementById('bunnyCall');
    if (callEl) { callEl.innerText = pred;
        callEl.style.color = callCol; }

    // ── Store for external use ──
    currentPrediction = { size: pred, confidence: conf, pattern: patKey };

    // ── Return result ──
    return {
        pattern: patKey,
        patternName: patName,
        prediction: pred,
        confidence: conf,
        message: msg
    };
}

// ── Export for Node.js / module usage ──
if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
    module.exports = {
        CPU_PATTERN_DB,
        BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES,
        runBunnyAI,
        numToType,
        lookupPattern,
        liveAdaptiveDetect
    };
}

// ── Also attach to window for browser usage ──

    window.runBunnyAI = runBunnyAI;
    window.CPU_PATTERN_DB = CPU_PATTERN_DB;
    window.BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES = BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES;
    window.currentPrediction = currentPrediction;
}

​Rules for Number Logic:
​If runBunnyAI predicts BIG: The target number must be randomly or logically chosen from [5, 6, 7, 8, 9].
​If runBunnyAI predicts SMALL: The target number must be from [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].
